  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：3D人体姿态估计后的生物力学属性预测；应用场景：康复、运动科学、工效学、临床运动分析；传感器类型：RGB视频（Human3.6M）；目标类别：人体17关节骨架。不涉及遥感场景、卫星图像、舰船/飞机/车辆检测。

Model architecture: BioModule：轻量级plug-in时序Transformer，可附加在任何3D姿态估计器下游，从标准17关节3D骨架预测生物力学属性。Estimator-agnostic设计，无需修改上游姿态模型。

Lightweight method: 论文自称"lightweight plug-in temporal transformer"，但摘要未说明具体轻量化手段（如参数量、FLOPs、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。轻量性体现在模块化插件设计，而非模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文面向的是地面生物力学分析应用，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Human3.6M（视频+3D关键点）与Human3.6Mplus（生物力学标签空间）对齐构建的大规模配对数据集；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在7种SOTA 3D姿态估计器上进行了基准测试，首次系统分析了上游姿态估计质量如何传播到下游生物力学预测保真度。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未提供具体轻量化指标（参数量、FLOPs等）；3. 仅针对人体姿态-生物力学场景，不具备遥感目标检测的可迁移性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的是其"plug-in模块化设计"思路——即在已有检测模型后附加轻量模块实现额外功能，但这种设计范式在遥感目标检测中并不新颖。整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无实质可借鉴点。

Abstract: Recent progress in 3D human pose estimation has made markerless recovery of skeletal motion increasingly accurate and scalable. However, most pose estimators remain optimized for geometric keypoint accuracy, while many real-world applications in rehabilitation, sports science, ergonomics, and clinical movement analysis require biomechanical quantities that describe how the body moves, loads, and activates. In this work, we propose BioModule, a lightweight plug-in temporal transformer that attaches downstream of any 3D pose estimator and predicts biomechanical attributes from standard 17-joint 3D skeletons. BioModule is estimator-agnostic and requires no modification of the upstream pose model, enabling existing pose estimators to be extended toward physically interpretable motion analysis.
  To train and evaluate BioModule, we construct a large-scale aligned dataset pairing Human3.6M video and 3D keypoints with the biomechanical label space of Human3.6Mplus. We establish and verify anatomical correspondence between coordinate systems of the two datasets, enabling frame-accurate cross-modal supervision. Using this aligned supervision, BioModule predicts biomechanical quantities. We further benchmark BioModule across seven state-of-the-art 3D pose estimators, providing the first systematic analysis of how upstream pose estimation quality propagates to downstream biomechanical prediction fidelity. The results position BioModule as a compact, modular bridge between vision-based pose estimation and biomechanically meaningful human motion analysis.

</details>


### [65] [WaspMOT: A Benchmark for Long-Term Multi-Object Tracking of Trichogramma Wasps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08729)
*Tomasz Stanczyk,Yuan Gao,Hardik Agarwal,Seongroo Yoon,Tiantao Zhang,Vincent Calcagno,Francois Bremond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个面向长时多目标跟踪的WaspMOT基准数据集，用于评估昆虫（赤眼蜂）在长时间序列中的身份保持能力，并测试了多种跟踪方法在该场景下的性能退化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于昆虫多目标跟踪（MOT）的长时身份保持问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测模型的轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、边缘部署等方向。虽然使用了检测器（tracking-by-detection），但核心贡献是数据集和长时关联分析，而非检测模型本身或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多目标跟踪（MOT），具体为长时身份保持（long-term identity preservation）。应用场景：生态学实验中赤眼蜂（Trichogramma wasps）的跟踪。传感器类型：普通可见光相机（非遥感传感器）。目标类别：昆虫（赤眼蜂个体）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构创新。测试了五种tracking-by-detection方法：ByteTrack、BoT-SORT、C-BIoU、OC-SORT、McByte，以及一个简单的空间轨迹拼接（spatial tracklet stitching）基线。这些均为现有方法，论文未提出新的检测或跟踪模型结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：WaspMOT（自建），包含10个序列，每个约12,000帧（8分钟以上，25 FPS），密集MOTChallenge格式标注，并提供oracle检测以隔离关联性能。评价指标：摘要未明确列出具体指标名称（如MOTA、IDF1等），但提到评估"trajectory fragmentation"（轨迹碎片化）。

Experiments: 在WaspMOT上评估了ByteTrack、BoT-SORT、C-BIoU、OC-SORT、McByte五种方法。所有方法均出现显著的轨迹碎片化（trajectory fragmentation），表明即使有完美检测，长时身份保持仍然困难。简单的空间轨迹拼接基线持续提升性能，说明仍有较大改进空间。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 数据集为特定生态场景（昆虫跟踪），场景单一，泛化性有限。3. 未讨论检测模型本身的改进或轻量化。4. 未提供模型效率指标（速度、参数量等）。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：（1）长时身份保持问题在遥感视频卫星目标跟踪中也可能存在（如长时间跟踪同一舰船/飞机），但论文方法（tracklet stitching）过于简单且场景差异大；（2）tracking-by-detection框架的检测器性能对跟踪质量的影响分析思路可迁移，但论文本身未深入讨论检测器设计。总体而言，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) has achieved strong performance on benchmarks dominated by short video sequences. However, such datasets do not adequately evaluate long-term identity preservation, where objects must be tracked consistently over extended durations. We introduce WaspMOT, a benchmark designed to address this gap through long-duration tracking of Trichogramma wasps in controlled ecological experiments. The dataset contains 10 sequences of approximately 12,000 frames each (over 8 minutes at 25 FPS), with dense MOTChallenge annotations and oracle detections to isolate association performance.
  Unlike existing benchmarks, WaspMOT forms a closed-set tracking scenario where all individuals remain present throughout the sequence, requiring consistent identity assignment across thousands of frames despite abrupt jumps, occlusions, and highly similar appearance. We establish a benchmark by evaluating five tracking-by-detection methods, including ByteTrack, BoT-SORT, C-BIoU, OC-SORT, and McByte, under a unified protocol. Results show that all methods suffer from significant trajectory fragmentation, highlighting the difficulty of long-term identity preservation even with perfect detections. A simple spatial tracklet stitching baseline consistently improves performance, indicating that substantial gains remain possible.
  WaspMOT provides a new benchmark for studying long-term association and reveals limitations of current tracking approaches that are not observable on conventional datasets. The benchmark will be made publicly available at the project repository: https://github.com/tstanczyk95/WaspMOT/ .

</details>


### [66] [OpenCoF: Learning to Reason Through Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08763)
*Xinyan Chen,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Dongzhi Jiang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OpenCoF框架，包含推理视频数据集OpenCoF-17K和微调视频生成模型Wan-CoF，探索通过时序帧进行推理（Chain-of-Frame）的方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于视频生成模型的推理能力（Chain-of-Frame reasoning），属于多模态/视频生成领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频生成推理（Chain-of-Frame reasoning）；应用场景：通用视频推理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Wan2.2-I2V-A14B视频生成模型微调得到Wan-CoF；引入视觉推理token和文本推理token分别捕获低层视觉线索和高层语义先验。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。基础模型Wan2.2-I2V-A14B为14B参数级别，体积巨大，完全不适用于星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：OpenCoF-17K（自建推理视频数据集，涵盖11个任务族）；评价指标：4个视频推理benchmark上的性能对比。

Experiments: Wan-CoF在4个视频推理benchmark上相比Wan2.2-I2V-A14B基线有显著提升；通过性能对比和注意力分析，研究了推理token在模型深度、去噪步数、空间和时间维度上的贡献。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；模型规模巨大（14B参数），不具备任何部署可行性；未涉及任何目标检测任务或遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于视频生成推理领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无交集。

Abstract: Reasoning has become a core capability for large models, especially when reliable decisions require understanding logical consequences. Recent video generation models offer a reasoning path distinct from previous Chain-of-Thought (CoT): reasoning can unfold through temporally connected frames, known as Chain-of-Frame (CoF) reasoning. However, existing video generators are primarily trained on general video corpora, still lacking diverse supervision and dedicated designs for CoF reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce OpenCoF, a framework comprising the OpenCoF-17K dataset, a reasoning video dataset spanning 11 task families, and Wan-CoF, a fine-tuned video model for studying whether diverse temporal supervision improves CoF behavior. Across four video reasoning benchmarks, Wan-CoF achieves considerable gains over the Wan2.2-I2V-A14B baseline. Building on this, we empirically explore more advanced designs for CoF capabilities, i.e., equipping the model with visual and textual reasoning tokens. This mechanism respectively captures low-level visual cues and high-level semantic priors for spatial and temporal reasoning. Through performance comparisons and attention analysis, we examine how these tokens contribute across model depth, denoising steps, space, and time. Our results suggest that stronger video reasoning requires both broad temporal supervision and explicit mechanisms for organizing intermediate reasoning state. We open-source the dataset, model, and code to facilitate future research on reasoning-oriented video generation.

</details>


### [67] [Enhancing In-context Panoramic Generation via Geometric-aware Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08765)
*Haoran Feng,Ruiyang Zhang,Longyi Zhang,Dizhe Zhang,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Canvas360是一个面向全景图像生成的两阶段框架，结合几何感知预训练与下游任务微调，并构建了100万对高质量全景数据集，用于风格迁移、修复、外扩和编辑等上下文生成任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于全景图像生成（panoramic generation），属于计算机视觉中的图像生成/编辑领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文聚焦全景图像生成，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：全景图像生成（panoramic generation），包括风格迁移、修复、外扩、编辑等上下文生成任务。应用场景：全景摄影/图像生成。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 两阶段框架（two-stage framework）：几何感知预训练 + 下游任务微调。关键模块：并行深度生成（parallel depth generation）、速度循环填充（velocity circular padding）、相似性损失正则化（similarity loss regularization）。通过token级拼接（token-level concatenation）实现统一上下文全景生成框架。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该工作面向通用图像生成，无星上/边缘部署考虑。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Canvas360Dataset，100万对高质量全景样本，覆盖风格迁移、修复、外扩、编辑任务。评价指标：全景专用FAED指标（panorama-specific FAED metric）及其他定量评估指标。

Experiments: 实验表明Canvas360提升了全景图像保真度，在FAED指标上表现突出，在多项定量评估中达到有竞争力或领先的结果。摘要未提供与基线方法的详细对比数据。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法；4. 未提供部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。其全景生成技术路线（几何感知预训练+下游微调）在遥感图像生成/数据增强领域可能有间接参考价值，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心目标不匹配。

Abstract: In this work, we present Canvas360, a two-stage framework for in-context panoramic generation that combines geometry-aware pretraining with downstream task-specific fine-tuning. To address the lack of large-scale, high-quality training data tailored to in-context panoramic tasks, we propose Canvas360Dataset, a collection of 1M high-quality paired panoramic samples for style transfer, inpainting, outpainting, and editing, enabling effective supervision across diverse in-context generation scenarios. On the modeling side, Canvas360 enhances text-to-panorama generation through parallel depth generation, velocity circular padding, and similarity loss regularization, enabling the model to learn geometry-aware representations, capture object distortion details, and improve geometric consistency and global coherence. Furthermore, empowered by strong panoramic priors, Canvas360 enables a unified in-context panoramic generation framework that supports diverse downstream tasks via token-level concatenation, surpassing prior methods in both task coverage and modeling flexibility. Extensive experiments show that Canvas360 improves panoramic image fidelity, achieving particularly strong performance on the panorama-specific FAED metric and competitive or leading results across the reported quantitative evaluations. More information can be found on our project page: https://zry000.github.io/Canvas360/

</details>


### [68] [OPSD-V: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Post-Training Few-Step Autoregressive Video Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08766)
*Hongyu Liu,Chun Wang,Feng Gao,Xuanhua He,Yue Ma,Ziyu Wan,Yong Zhang,Xiaoming Wei,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPSD-V，一种用于后训练少步自回归视频扩散模型的自蒸馏范式，通过引入真实长视频作为时序上下文提供密集轨迹级监督，减少长程退化。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于视频扩散模型的自蒸馏方法，属于生成式AI/视频生成领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：少步自回归视频扩散模型的后训练蒸馏。场景：长视频生成。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于自回归（AR）视频扩散模型，具体应用于Self-Forcing和LongLive两种代表性模型。学生模型遵循推理时自回归展开，教师模型使用更干净的AR一致性时序缓存（用真实视频替换旧历史）。

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏（self-distillation），但蒸馏目标不是模型轻量化/压缩，而是提升生成质量、减少长程误差累积。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、实时推理或边缘部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注生成质量而非推理效率或部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 使用VBenchLong评分评估长视频质量。用户研究：10名参与者，20对视频，偏好判断。具体训练数据集摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在Self-Forcing和LongLive模型上应用OPSD-V，在视觉质量、运动动态和VBenchLong分数上取得一致改进。用户研究中66.0%总体偏好（排除平局后82.5%）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。摘要未讨论计算效率、模型大小、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。蒸馏方法（on-policy self-distillation）的设计思路——即让学生模拟推理时行为、教师提供更干净的监督信号——在概念上可迁移到遥感目标检测模型的蒸馏中，但论文本身不涉及检测任务或遥感场景，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: We propose OPSD-V, an on-policy self-distillation paradigm for post-training few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. Existing few-step AR video generators can produce long videos with low latency, but still suffer from error accumulation and weakened motion dynamics during long autoregressive rollout. OPSD-V reduces long-horizon degradation while preserving the original few-step inference path. The key idea is to introduce real long-video data as temporal context during training and use it to provide dense trajectory-level supervision. Specifically, the student follows the exact inference-time rollout, generating each chunk conditioned on its own previously generated KV cache. In parallel, the teacher is evaluated at the same student-visited denoising states, but uses a cleaner AR-consistent temporal cache in which older history can be replaced by real-video context. This provides dense denoising-level corrective targets under on-policy AR cache dynamics, without changing the sampler, number of denoising steps, or inference-time cache mechanism. We apply OPSD-V to representative few-step AR video models, including Self-Forcing and LongLive. Experiments show consistent improvements in visual quality, motion dynamics, and VBenchLong scores. A user study with 10 participants comparing 20 video pairs shows that OPSD-V is preferred over the base models in 66.0% of overall-preference judgments (82.5% excluding ties).

</details>


### [69] [Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning for Panoramic Outdoor Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08769)
*Weijian Chen,Weibo Yao,Yuhang Zhang,Xiaolin Tang,Guo Wang,Weijun Zhang,Xitong Gao,Yihao Chen,Hongde Qin,Lu Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向大规模户外场景的全景3D高斯泼溅重建框架PanoLOG，包含基于几何与梯度的分区策略(G²PS)和首个大规模全景数据集Pano360，实现块并行训练与高质量渲染。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于3D场景重建（3D Gaussian Splatting）和全景图像渲染，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然"大规模户外场景"与遥感有场景上的表面关联，但核心任务是NeRF/3DGS类的新视角合成与重建，而非遥感图像中的目标检测或语义理解。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大规模户外场景3D重建（3D Gaussian Splatting）；应用场景：全景图像驱动的室外场景重建；传感器类型：全景相机（equirectangular projection, ERP）；目标类别：无目标检测任务，涉及场景几何与渲染质量。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等传感器类型。

Model architecture: 两阶段粗到细框架PanoLOG：全局粗阶段（sky-sphere modeling + panoramic monocular depth supervision）+ 精化阶段（G²PS: Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy，基于视差驱动不确定性的自适应包围体 + 基于梯度重要性评分的相机分配）。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。文中提到的"block-parallel training"是训练策略层面的并行化，而非推理阶段的模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是训练阶段的可扩展性和块并行训练，而非推理阶段的星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 自建数据集：Pano360（首个大规模全景户外场景重建基准数据集）；评价指标：rendering quality（渲染质量，具体指标如PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS等摘要未明确列出）；实验设置：大规模户外场景重建。

Experiments: G²PS在渲染质量上达到SOTA，且支持可扩展的块并行训练。摘要未提供与其他方法的详细定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法；4. 未提供推理阶段的效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的间接启发：G²PS中的"基于梯度重要性评分"思想或许可以迁移到轻量化中的结构化剪枝（根据梯度重要性筛选通道或层），但需要大量改造且与遥感目标检测场景差异巨大。不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Scaling 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to large outdoor scenes is costly in both data acquisition and computation. Adopting panoramic images with equirectangular projection (ERP) can reduce capture effort via their full $360^{\circ}$ field of view, yet the resulting omnipresent visibility invalidates existing partitioning strategies that rely on local camera frustums, causing block-wise optimization to degenerate into global training. Thus, we propose PanoLOG, a two-stage coarse-to-fine framework equipped with a Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy tailored for large-scale panoramic 3DGS reconstruction. In the global coarse stage, PanoLOG leverages sky-sphere modeling and panoramic monocular depth supervision for reliable geometry, while in the refinement stage, G$^2$PS builds adaptive bounding volumes via parallax-driven uncertainty and assigns cameras via gradient-based importance scoring. Furthermore, we construct Pano360, the first benchmark on large-scale panoramic dataset for outdoor scene reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G$^2$PS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while maintaining scalable, block-parallel training. Our models, training code, and dataset are publicly available.

</details>


### [70] [LongE2V: Long-Horizon Event-based Video Reconstruction, Prediction, and Frame Interpolation with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08770)
*Cheng-De Fan,Chun-Wei Tuan Mu,Chen-Wei Chang,Chin-Yang Lin,Kun-Ru Wu,Yu-Chee Tseng,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LongE2V方法，利用预训练视频扩散先验，联合处理基于事件的视频重建、预测和帧插值任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是基于事件相机(event camera)的稀疏事件流到高质量视频的重建，属于事件视觉(event-based vision)和视频生成领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：事件流到视频的重建、预测、帧插值；场景：通用事件相机视觉；传感器：事件相机(event camera)；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于预训练视频扩散模型(video diffusion priors)进行微调，引入Autoregressive Unrolling（自回归展开）、Adaptive Context Switching（自适应上下文切换）、Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction（重编码对齐与交叉残差校正）等模块。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。基于扩散模型的方法通常计算开销极大，不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 在真实世界基准数据集(real-world benchmarks)上实验，具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标涉及感知质量(perceptual quality)、时间一致性(temporal coherence)、零样本泛化(zero-shot generalization)等，未提及mAP、FPS等目标检测常用指标。

Experiments: 在视频重建、预测、帧插值三个任务上均优于现有方法(state-of-the-art)，表现出优异的时间一致性和零样本泛化能力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测完全无关；2. 基于扩散模型，计算量巨大，不具备轻量化特征；3. 未提供任何部署相关指标；4. 仅针对事件相机数据，不适用于光学/SAR遥感图像。

Ideas for my research: 基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来研究遥感视频卫星的事件流数据（如事件相机在轨应用），其视频扩散先验和长序列稳定性方法可能有参考价值，但与本课题"遥感目标检测模型轻量化"方向相距甚远。

Abstract: Recovering high-quality video from sparse event streams is a challenging task. Regression methods often blur textures, while existing generative models struggle with long-term stability. We propose LongE2V, a novel approach that leverages pre-trained video diffusion priors to jointly handle event-based video reconstruction, prediction, and frame interpolation. By fine-tuning a foundational video model, our approach achieves high data efficiency and superior perceptual quality. We introduce Autoregressive Unrolling and Adaptive Context Switching to mitigate temporal drift in extremely long sequences. We also propose Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction to ensure precise bidirectional consistency during frame interpolation. Furthermore, Event Voxel Density Augmentation ensures robustness across varying sensor resolutions. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that LongE2V outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all three tasks, exhibiting exceptional temporal coherence and zero-shot generalization. Project page: https://cdfan0627.github.io/LongE2V-page/

</details>


### [71] [ZipDepth: Bringing Lightweight Zero-Shot Monocular Depth Anywhere, on Any Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08771)
*Fabio Tosi,Luca Bartolomei,Matteo Poggi,Stefano Mattoccia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZipDepth是一个仅6.1M参数的轻量级单目深度估计网络，通过重参数化编码器-解码器和大规模多域知识蒸馏，在零样本泛化与部署效率之间取得最佳平衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于monocular depth estimation（单目深度估计），而非遥感目标检测。虽然涉及轻量化（6.1M参数、实时推理）和知识蒸馏，但任务类型（深度估计vs目标检测）和应用场景（通用场景vs遥感卫星）均不匹配。没有涉及遥感图像、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关元素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，仅轻量化方法（知识蒸馏+重参数化）有微弱借鉴意义，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：单目深度估计（monocular depth estimation）；场景：通用场景（multi-domain），非遥感场景；传感器：RGB相机；目标类别：无（深度估计任务）。

Model architecture: 重参数化编码器-解码器（reparameterizable encoder-decoder），通过大规模知识蒸馏从foundation model（基础模型）学习。

Lightweight method: 轻量网络设计（重参数化编码器-解码器）+ 大规模知识蒸馏（large-scale knowledge distillation from a foundation model）。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、NAS等具体压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 参数量6.1M，可在服务器GPU到功耗受限设备上实现实时推理（real-time rates）。摘要未说明具体FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等详细部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 5个基准数据集（five benchmarks）上进行零样本评估。摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 在5个基准上，ZipDepth在零样本准确率与部署效率之间取得最佳权衡（best trade-off），精度接近参数量大50倍的基础模型。摘要未提供具体数值对比和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 任务为深度估计，与遥感目标检测任务完全不同；2. 未涉及遥感场景下的域适应问题；3. 摘要未说明在边缘设备上的具体延迟、功耗等实测数据；4. 未讨论在轨部署相关约束（如辐射环境、存储限制等）。

Ideas for my research: 1. 知识蒸馏思路可借鉴：从大模型蒸馏到轻量模型，可尝试将遥感大模型（如遥感基础模型）蒸馏到轻量检测网络；2. 重参数化编码器-解码器设计思路可用于轻量遥感目标检测网络设计；3. 多域训练策略可借鉴用于遥感多传感器（光学、SAR、多光谱）的域泛化检测。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has seen remarkable progress through foundation models achieving robust zero-shot generalization, yet their computational demands place them far beyond the reach of embedded and mobile platforms. Lightweight alternatives exist, but have been developed almost exclusively within single-domain, self-supervised paradigms, failing silently under domain shift. We present ZipDepth, a compact monocular depth network that bridges this gap by combining an efficient reparameterizable encoder-decoder with large-scale knowledge distillation from a foundation model over a large multi-domain training set. Comprising just 6.1M parameters, ZipDepth runs at real-time rates from server GPUs to power-constrained devices, achieving the best trade-off between zero-shot accuracy and deployment efficiency among lightweight models across five benchmarks, taking a significant step towards the accuracy of foundation models with 50x more parameters.

</details>


### [72] [Wat3R: Underwater 3D Geometry Learning without Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08772)
*Jiangwei Ren,Xingyu Jiang,Zijie Song,Wei Xu,Hongkai Lin,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Wat3R，一种跨域半监督学习框架，利用教师-学生架构和无标注水下视频实现从空气到水下场景的3D重建，无需水下标注数据。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦水下3D重建（underwater 3D reconstruction），不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测（object detection）或小目标/旋转框/舰船/飞机检测；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。除非对跨域半监督学习在遥感无标注数据利用上有兴趣，可作为旁路参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：水下多视图深度估计与点云重建（underwater multi-view depth estimation and point cloud reconstruction）；场景：水下环境（underwater environments），非遥感/卫星场景；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 教师-学生架构（teacher-student architecture），前馈式3D重建模型（feed-forward 3D reconstruction models），跨域半监督学习框架（cross-domain semi-supervised learning framework）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏（虽然用了teacher-student但目的是半监督学习而非模型压缩）、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 自建数据集Water3D，覆盖多种水体和水下场景；评价指标：多视图深度估计和点云重建指标（具体指标名称摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在Water3D数据集上，Wat3R优于当前最先进的水下多视图深度估计和点云重建方法。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景，领域差异大；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；4. 教师-学生架构虽与知识蒸馏有形式相似性，但本文用于跨域半监督学习而非模型压缩，不可直接类比。

Ideas for my research: 1. 跨域半监督学习（cross-domain semi-supervised learning）的思想可借鉴：在遥感在轨场景中，可考虑利用大量无标注遥感数据辅助有监督训练，减少对标注数据的依赖；2. 交叉视图一致性损失（cross-view consistency loss）的设计思路可用于遥感多视角目标检测中的特征融合；3. 但整体而言，本文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点非常有限。

Abstract: Estimating 3D geometry in underwater environments presents unique challenges due to light attenuation, scattering, and the absence of large-scale, high-quality 3D annotations. Pioneering methods rely on massive dense annotations that are impractical in underwater settings. In this paper, we propose Wat3R, a cross-domain semi-supervised learning framework designed to adapt feed-forward 3D reconstruction models from air to underwater scenes. Uniquely, our method eliminates the need for any annotated underwater data following a teacher-student architecture, that learns robust geometry representations merely on abundant unlabeled real underwater video footage. We also design a cross-view consistency loss that leverages geometric cues from other views to compensate for the information degradation in the current view caused by water attenuation and scattering. Furthermore, considering the lack of comprehensive evaluation benchmarks, we construct Water3D, a diverse dataset covering various water bodies and underwater scenarios, designed for geometric task evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that Wat3R outperforms current state-of-the-art methods in underwater multi-view depth estimation and point cloud reconstruction. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R .

</details>

### [73] [Projected Energy Matching for Generative 3D Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07749)
*Daniel Barco,Michal Balcerak,Suprosanna Shit,Chinmay Prabhakar,Philipp Denzel,Bjoern Menze,Frank-Peter Schilling*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出Projected Energy Matching框架，通过Helmholtz蒸馏和负缓存策略解决能量匹配模型在3D高维数据上的训练瓶颈，应用于医学CT稀疏视角重建。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于生成式能量模型（Energy-Based Models）的改进，应用于医学CT逆问题（稀疏视角重建），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不推荐阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生成式建模（能量匹配框架）；应用场景：医学CT稀疏视角重建；传感器类型：CT（医学影像）；目标类别：无（生成任务，非检测）。完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测。

Model architecture: Projected Energy Matching框架，包含：Helmholtz蒸馏（用Hutchinson迹估计器吸收旋转噪声到辅助残差网络）、负缓存策略（Negative Caching，跨微批次复用负样本）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。文中提到的"减少计算量"是指相比标准能量匹配的训练成本降低，而非模型压缩/加速/部署相关的轻量化技术（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"amortized pipeline reduces total compute to a small fraction"，但这是训练阶段的计算减少，非推理部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅提及应用场景为"real-world medical CT inverse problems, specifically sparse-view reconstruction"。

Experiments: 摘要未提供具体实验结果数值。仅声称方法在CT重建中实现高保真度并解决严重测量伪影。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景，与本研究方向无直接关联；2. 未提供任何轻量化部署相关的量化指标；3. 方法针对生成式建模，而非判别式检测任务；4. 实验仅在医学CT上验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. Helmholtz蒸馏中利用Hutchinson迹估计器处理非保守场噪声的思路，或许可类比到遥感检测模型中处理噪声/伪影；2. 负缓存策略（Negative Caching）在训练中复用样本的思想，可能对遥感数据稀缺场景下的训练加速有启发。但整体方向差异过大，不建议深入。

Abstract: Energy Matching has emerged as a powerful generative framework that combines flow model efficiency with the explicit likelihood of Energy-Based Models (EBMs) via a single, time-independent scalar potential. However, directly training this potential on high-dimensional 3D data remains computationally challenging. While distilling a pre-trained flow model circumvents some of the initial training costs, we demonstrate that velocity fields inevitably contain non-conservative rotational artifacts (curl). Forcing a strictly conservative scalar potential to match this unconstrained field creates a "structural conflict", which degrades generation quality and mode coverage. To solve this, we propose Projected Energy Matching, a scalable framework that resolves these structural and computational bottlenecks. We introduce Helmholtz Distillation, a structural relaxation that leverages a Hutchinson trace estimator to explicitly absorb rotational noise into an auxiliary residual network. We subsequently refine this landscape using Negative Caching, a memory-efficient strategy that reuses negative samples across micro-batches, rendering sampling tractable during contrastive training with gradient accumulation. We deploy our method as an unconditional prior for real-world medical CT inverse problems, specifically sparse-view reconstruction. Ultimately, our amortized pipeline reduces total compute to a small fraction of that required by standard energy matching, while achieving high-fidelity reconstructions and successfully resolving severe measurement artifacts.

</details>

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标为重建质量(reconstruction quality)和擦除概率下的鲁棒性。

Experiments: 实验对比了空域JSCC和特征域JSCC在不同均匀/非均匀擦除概率下的表现，空域擅长处理随机局部损失，特征域对分布式擦除更鲁棒。

Abstract: This paper investigates semantic-aware neural joint source-channel coding (JSCC) for robust video transmission over block erasure channels. We propose a neural video compression framework exploring both spatial-domain and feature-domain designs. In the spatial domain, video frames are partitioned into blocks, enabling localized erasure handling and fine-grained robustness control via uniform erasure and two-level, semantic-guided non-uniform erasure strategies. In the feature domain, latent features are partitioned, enabling missing features to be semantically recovered while maintaining overall spatial consistency. Comprehensive experiments quantify reconstruction quality under varying uniform and non-uniform erasure probabilities. Our results show that spatial-domain JSCC excels at handling random localized losses, whereas feature-domain JSCC provides superior robustness to distributed erasures and maintains fidelity under low-loss scenarios. The analysis highlights the trade-offs between spatial continuity and semantic redundancy, offering insights for designing robust, task-aware video communication systems.

</details>


### [75] [False Confidence: Automated Labels Confound Fairness Audits in Cervical Spine Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07852)
*Linus Juni,Aasa Feragen,Aditya Parikh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文研究颈椎MRI分割中的公平性审计问题，关注银标签（机器生成标签）对模型性能评估和公平性判定的偏差影响，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学图像分割（cervical-spine MRI segmentation）；应用场景：临床工作流中的公平性审计；传感器类型：MRI（磁共振成像），非遥感传感器；目标类别：颈椎结构（非遥感目标如舰船/飞机/车辆）。

Experiments: 主要发现：模型在人口统计学上是公平的，但参考标签（银标签 vs 金标签）的选择会引入偏差；使用银标签评估会高估性能约8个Dice点，并改变公平性结论（年龄因素从非显著变为显著）；提出了"false magnitude"（虚假幅度）和"false confidence"（虚假置信度）两个概念。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法直接评估其在遥感轻量化方面的局限性。该论文的局限性在于仅针对颈椎MRI分割场景，且未讨论如何解决银标签偏差问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。但可间接注意：在遥感目标检测中，如果使用自动标注（如伪标签）或合成数据训练轻量化模型，需警惕标签质量对性能评估和公平性的影响——这一点可作为方法论上的警示，但非核心借鉴。

Abstract: Automated segmentation of cervical-spine MRI is increasingly used in clinical workflows, yet no fairness audit exists for this anatomy. We show that auditing these segmentation tasks is complicated by a common property of modern segmentation datasets: expert-annotated gold labels are expensive, so abundant machine-generated (silver) labels are added to limit annotation cost. This matters because the reference used to judge a model can itself be biased. In this study, we present the first fairness audit of cervical-spine MRI segmentation across sex, age, and race using the CSpineSeg dataset. We observe that the deployed model is demographically fair, but the choice of reference label, however, is not neutral. Because a dataset's silver labels are generated by a model trained on its gold labels, any new model trained on those same gold labels agrees more with the silver labels than with expert truth: scoring identical predictions against silver rather than gold overestimates performance by ~8 Dice points and turns the fairness verdict for age from non-significant to significant - not by the gap inflation Parikh et al. report (which we term false magnitude) but by collapsing within-group variance (which we term false confidence). Reference-label provenance is thus a first-order confounder in segmentation evaluation: performance and fairness should be reported against expert labels, and any fairness claim stated together with the provenance of its reference.

</details>


### [76] [SCI-Mamba: Unsupervised Learning based Low-Light Image Enhancement for Non-Cooperative Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08033)
*Yiyong Sun,Weihang Shan,Shijun Wei,Diwei Zhou,Guang Zhai*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出SCI-Mamba，一种面向在轨非合作航天器低光观测的无监督增强网络，结合自校准学习、VMamba架构和Retinex先验，并构建了Space Dark-1.0低光航天器数据集。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及在轨（on-orbit）航天器观测和星上硬件资源受限场景，与遥感卫星在轨智能有一定交集，但核心任务是低光图像增强（low-light enhancement），而非目标检测（object detection），也不涉及模型轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）。属于视觉增强方向，对目标检测轻量化课题仅有间接参考价值。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关。论文聚焦低光增强而非目标检测，且轻量化仅停留在架构层面（VMamba线性复杂度），缺乏量化指标。可作为背景参考，但非核心文献。

Model architecture: SCI-Mamba框架：自校准无监督学习（self-calibrated unsupervised learning）+ VMamba架构（线性复杂度状态空间模型）+ Retinex物理先验。VMamba替代了传统CNN和Transformer，具有线性计算复杂度。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。仅提到VMamba架构具有线性复杂度（linear-complexity），以及"lightweight enhancement pipeline adaptable to resource-limited spaceborne hardware"，但未给出具体轻量化技术细节或压缩比。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到"adaptable to resource-limited spaceborne hardware"和"inference speed"优势，但未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅定性说明推理速度优于CNN、Transformer和Mamba方法。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Space Dark-1.0（自建低光航天器数据集），包含真实轨道影像、暗室硬件在环测量和物理约束合成数据，覆盖多种光照、运动和姿态条件。评价指标：视觉真实性（visual authenticity）、色彩保真度（color fidelity）、推理速度（inference speed）。未说明具体数值指标如PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS等。

Experiments: 与CNN-based、Transformer-based和Mamba-based方法进行全面比较，验证了SCI-Mamba在视觉真实性、色彩保真度和推理速度上的优势。消融实验和详细数值结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 核心任务是低光增强而非目标检测，不涉及检测精度（mAP等）指标；2. 未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟等量化轻量化指标，无法判断实际星上部署可行性；3. 数据集为航天器场景，非典型遥感地物目标（舰船、飞机、车辆等）；4. 无监督方法在极端光照条件下的鲁棒性有待验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. VMamba架构（线性复杂度状态空间模型）可作为轻量化检测骨干网络的候选方案，替代传统CNN/Transformer，降低计算复杂度；2. 自校准无监督学习策略可借鉴到遥感目标检测的域适应或低光场景适应中；3. Retinex物理先验与深度学习结合的思路可用于遥感图像预处理增强模块；4. 构建专用数据集（含真实+半实物仿真+合成数据）的策略值得借鉴。

Abstract: Low-light visual perception acts as the core visual foundation for on-orbit servicing missions targeting non-cooperative spacecraft, supporting autonomous rendezvous, pose estimation, component detection and robotic capture operations. Spaceborne imagery suffers from severe low-light degradation, while the extreme scarcity of paired normal/low-light space samples severely limits the generalization capacity of supervised enhancement algorithms. To address this practical bottleneck, this paper proposes SCI-Mamba, an unsupervised enhancement network for low-light orbital spacecraft observations. The proposed framework unites self-calibrated unsupervised learning, linear-complexity VMamba architecture and Retinex physical priors, delivering a lightweight enhancement pipeline adaptable to resource-limited spaceborne hardware. We construct Space Dark-1.0, a dedicated low-light spacecraft dataset integrating real orbital footage, darkroom hardware-in-the-loop measurements and physically constrained synthetic data covering diverse illumination, motion and attitude conditions. Comprehensive comparisons with CNN-, Transformer- and prevailing Mamba-based approaches verify the advantages of SCI-Mamba in visual authenticity, color fidelity and inference speed. The proposed framework provides a practical low-light enhancement solution for close-proximity non-cooperative space operations.
  The code is available at https://github.com/bitswh/SCI-Mamba

</details>

Experiments: 在5个2D医学影像数据集、171个影像组学目标上，ConRad相比基线方法在保持近名义覆盖率的同时提升了特征级区间效率。消融实验表明分割边界不确定性特征是区间效率的最大贡献者。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对2D医学影像，未验证在遥感图像上的适用性；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 未讨论计算效率或模型轻量化；4. 共形预测框架的实时推理开销未说明。

Abstract: Radiomic features derived from medical images and segmentation masks are used to support decision making in clinical imaging pipelines. In practice, these features are often computed from predicted masks, but segmentation models can be overconfident or poorly calibrated, making derived measurements appear more reliable than they are. Conformal prediction (CP) provides distribution-free prediction intervals with finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees, but black-box intervals for segmentation-derived radiomics can be inefficient because they ignore test-time information about image appearance, mask geometry, and segmentation uncertainty. We propose ConRad, a conformal framework for scalar radiomic targets that uses covariates derived from the predicted mask, input image, predicted radiomics, and boundary uncertainty to construct adaptive intervals while maintaining coverage. Across five 2D medical imaging datasets and 171 retained radiomic targets, we show that ConRad improves feature-level efficiency compared to baselines while maintaining near-nominal empirical coverage. Ablation results further indicate that segmentation boundary uncertainty features are the largest contributors to interval efficiency.

</details>


### [78] [Partial-Reference IQA Based on Hermite-Gauss Structural Prediction and Texture Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08563)
*Paolo Giannitrapani,Elio D. Di Claudio,Giovanni Jacovitti*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出PreSPA，一种部分参考图像质量评估框架，通过结构感知和纹理敏感两个互补指标分解感知质量，仅从参考图像提取一个标量先验μ，实现极低参考信息依赖的高效质量评估。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是图像质量评估(IQA)问题，而非目标检测；虽然IQA可作为遥感图像预处理的一环，但摘要未涉及遥感场景、卫星图像、目标检测或模型轻量化核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非课题涉及遥感图像质量评估作为预处理环节，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图像质量评估（IQA），具体为部分参考(Partial-Reference)质量评估。场景：通用图像质量评估，非遥感特定。传感器类型：未说明。目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: PreSPA框架包含两个互补指标：(1) 结构感知指标(No-Reference方式)，通过Hermite-Gauss预测失真梯度场及其曲率角方差来捕获结构退化；(2) 纹理敏感指标，通过标量先验μ估计局部噪声。最终分数通过仅含三个可解释参数的仿射融合得到。

Lightweight method: 方法本身具有轻量特性：仅从参考图像提取一个标量μ，将参考信息压缩到信息论最小值；仿射融合仅含三个可解释参数，无需数据集特定校准；计算高效。但这不是针对目标检测模型的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等），而是IQA方法自身的紧凑设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提到"computationally efficient"（计算高效）和"compact"（紧凑），但缺乏量化数据。未涉及星上/边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等硬件加速。

Datasets and metrics: 在六个标准IQA基准数据集上评估。评价指标：摘要未具体说明使用的IQA指标（如PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS等）。对比方法：与领先的No-Reference方法和Full-Reference方法对比。

Experiments: 在六个标准基准上，PreSPA持续达到或超越领先的No-Reference方法，在多个情况下匹配Full-Reference模型的精度。消融实验：摘要未说明。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；(2) 仅针对通用图像质量评估，未在遥感图像上验证；(3) 缺乏参数量、计算量等量化效率指标；(4) 未说明在失真类型多样性上的鲁棒性边界。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：(1) 其"仅提取最小参考信息"的思路可类比于知识蒸馏中教师模型信息的压缩传递；(2) 结构感知+纹理敏感的分解思路可用于遥感图像预处理质量筛选；(3) 三个可解释参数的仿射融合设计思路可用于轻量级后处理模块。但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接关联度很低。

Abstract: We propose PreSPA (Partial-Reference Structural Prediction Approach), a Partial-Reference Image Quality Assessment framework that decomposes perceptual quality into two complementary indices. A structure-aware index, operating in a No-Reference manner, captures structural degradation through Hermite-Gauss prediction of the distorted gradient field and the angular variance of its curvature. A texture-sensitive index estimates local noise through a scalar prior $μ$, obtained from energy differences between reference and distorted complex gradient maps on strong-edge regions and accumulated over weakly-structured ones, reflecting the perceptual leakage of degraded edges into surrounding textures. Crucially, $μ$ is the only information extracted from the reference and is computed once per image pair, reducing the reference footprint to its information-theoretic minimum. The final score is produced by an affine fusion with only three interpretable parameters, making the method compact, transparent, and computationally efficient, with the viewing distance embedded into the operator scale and no dataset-specific calibration. Extensive evaluations on six standard benchmarks show that PreSPA consistently rivals or exceeds leading No-Reference approaches, while in several cases matching the accuracy of Full-Reference models.

</details>

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦企业AI代理的主动式信息推送，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等压缩加速技术。属于企业级NLP/知识图谱应用，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：企业AI主动式信息推送（proactive information surfacing）；应用场景：合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应、销售管道管理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Context Graph（实时关系数据结构）+ Delta Detection Engine（状态变化监测）+ Proactivity Scorer（紧迫性/相关性/角色匹配评分）+ LLM驱动的Surfacing Layer（排名通知与解释生成）。使用NetworkX和Anthropic Claude API实现。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗、延迟等部署相关信息。仅提到mean time to surface从47分钟降至30秒以下，但这是企业场景下的信息推送延迟，非模型推理延迟。

Datasets and metrics: 三个企业案例研究（合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应、销售管道管理）；评价指标：Precision@5（0.83）、False Positive Rate（0.11）、Mean Time to Surface（从47分钟降至30秒以下）。无遥感相关数据集。

Experiments: 在三个企业场景中验证，Precision@5达到0.83，FPR为0.11，信息推送时间从47分钟（反应式基线）降至30秒以下。无消融实验或与遥感检测方法的对比。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化任何一方面；2. 仅在企业文本数据上验证，无视觉任务实验；3. 依赖LLM（Claude API），推理成本高，不适合星上部署；4. 未讨论模型压缩、边缘推理等部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：Context Graph中"状态变化监测"（Delta Detection）的思想，如果类比到遥感时序变化检测任务中，可能有一定概念上的参考价值，但方法本身（基于NLP的实体关系图）与遥感视觉检测差异巨大，不推荐深入借鉴。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice. Organising alignment in this way yields a construct we call alignment plausibility - a structured demonstration that a system's values, training regime, and oversight mechanisms are together consistent with safe and positive outcomes. We propose alignment plausibility as a regulatory construct (by drawing analogy to the established construct of biological plausibility) for AI in health: a principled way to argue for, or against, trust that systems are aligned to positive health outcomes, will cause no harm even where capable of doing so, and will ultimately lead to patient benefit.

</details>

Abstract: The human body is at the center of a growing family of technologies designed to tightly and persistently couple biological and digital systems. Robotic prostheses are a representative example of this tight coupling. Also referred to as bionic limbs, robotic prostheses are devices that support people who have lost limbs in pursuing daily life activities such as walking and grasping objects. Bionic limbs are now perceptive and responsive owing to their integration with advanced sensors and artificial intelligence-based control approaches. Consequently, such robotic prostheses can now be viewed as semiautonomous wearable robotic systems that can co-adapt with their users. However, the same sensing and control advancements that increase the capability of robotic prostheses also introduce threat vectors that could be exploited by malicious entities to violate the privacy of users. To fully realize the benefits of next-generation bionic limbs, we maintain it is important to directly understand and address these privacy risks and the barriers they might present to user adoption. This paper therefore introduces a new line of inquiry we term idiobionics to holistically investigate issues at the intersection of privacy and intelligent bionic limbs. As the main contribution of this paper, we define idiobionics, ground it in related literature, and provide preliminary evidence showing and discussing potential adversarial attacks that could exploit intelligent bionic limb designs. We then contribute a curated list of open research questions within idiobionics that are relevant to researchers in wearable robotics and other human-facing autonomous systems. We expect that idiobionics research will help unlock the full potential of robotic prostheses and related bionic devices.

</details>


### [85] [Infinity-Parser2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07836)
*Zuming Huang,Jun Huang,Kexuan Ren,Baode Wang,Weizhen Li,Jianming Feng,Yu Wang,Yichen Yao,Shijun Lin,Yige Tang,Cheng Peng,Weidi Xu,Wei Chu,Yinghui Xu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向文档解析的大规模多模态模型，通过可控数据合成管道和多任务强化学习实现端到端解析，并发布5M双语语料库和两个变体模型。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于文档解析（document parsing），涉及OCR、版面分析、表格/公式/图表解析等，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测中的遥感特定目标（舰船/飞机/车辆等）。虽然模型包含目标检测相关能力（element bounding boxes），但应用场景是文档而非遥感。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，阅读优先级极低。除非对数据合成管道或多任务RL框架有跨领域借鉴需求，否则不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：文档解析（document parsing）、版面分析、表格/公式/图表解析、文档VQA。应用场景：通用文档处理。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：文档元素（文本框、表格、公式、图表、化学式等）。

Model architecture: 大语言多模态模型（Large Multimodal Model），具体架构细节摘要未说明。发布两个变体：Infinity-Parser2-Flash（低延迟优化）和Infinity-Parser2-Pro（高精度优化）。

Lightweight method: 摘要提到Infinity-Parser2-Flash变体通过某种方式实现了3.68倍吞吐量提升（over Infinity-Parser-7B），但具体轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等）摘要未说明。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署关键指标。仅提到Flash变体有3.68× throughput gain，但未给出绝对延迟或推理速度数值。与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建Infinity-Doc2-5M（5M样本，中英双语，含多种文档类型标注）。评测基准：olmOCR-Bench（87.6%）、ParseBench（74.3%）。对比方法：DeepSeek-OCR-2、PaddleOCR-VL-1.5、MinerU2.5。

Experiments: Infinity-Parser2-Pro在olmOCR-Bench上达到87.6%，在ParseBench上达到74.3%，超越对比方法。Flash变体有3.68倍吞吐量提升。消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感场景，无法直接用于遥感目标检测；2. 轻量化方法细节未披露，仅提及吞吐量提升倍数；3. 未给出模型参数量、FLOPs等关键效率指标；4. 文档解析与遥感目标检测的任务范式差异大（密集文本识别 vs. 稀疏目标定位分类）。

Ideas for my research: 1. 数据合成管道（controllable data-synthesis pipeline + iterative refinement loop）的思路可借鉴到遥感领域，用于生成带旋转框/方向标注的遥感目标检测合成数据；2. 多任务强化学习联合优化多个目标的框架设计思路，可迁移到遥感多任务学习（如检测+分割+场景分类）；3. Flash变体的低延迟优化思路（具体方法需查阅全文）可能对星上推理有参考价值，但需确认具体技术手段。

Abstract: We present Infinity-Parser2, a large multimodal model that couples a controllable data-synthesis pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning for end-to-end document parsing, addressing the persistent scarcity of faithfully annotated parsing corpora. Our contributions are threefold. First, we build a scalable synthesis engine, pairing a controllable rendering framework with an iterative refinement loop, and use it to construct and open-source Infinity-Doc2-5M: a 5-million-sample bilingual (Chinese/English) corpus spanning diverse document types, annotated with element bounding boxes, canonical content forms (Markdown, HTML, LaTeX, SMILES, structured charts), and full-page reading order. Second, we introduce a verifiable, multi-task reward system that enables Joint Reinforcement Learning across eight co-trained objectives (document parsing, layout analysis, table parsing, math formula parsing, chart parsing, chemical formula parsing, document VQA, and general multimodal understanding), unifying perception, structure, and reasoning in a single optimization signal. Third, we release two variants under a shared architecture: Infinity-Parser2-Flash, optimized for low-latency inference with a $3.68\times$ throughput gain over Infinity-Parser-7B, and Infinity-Parser2-Pro, engineered for precision-critical settings. Infinity-Parser2-Pro reaches state-of-the-art 87.6% on olmOCR-Bench and 74.3% on ParseBench, surpassing DeepSeek-OCR-2, PaddleOCR-VL-1.5, and MinerU2.5, with strong generalization to charts, chemical formulas, and document VQA.

</details>

Abstract: VectorizationLLM is a specialized Large Language Model based on Google open-weight LLMs. The model is designed to assist students to learn smart vectorization, time/wave vector analysis, piecewise functions, Fourier analysis, and differential equations in MATLAB. The course application is CTEC 247: Applied Computational Analysis II by the Department of Electrical & Computer Engineering Technology at New York Institute of Technology Old Westbury. The LLM model is designed to be an instructive assistant, providing detailed explanations of concepts with examples from in-class notes without providing direct answers to questions. The model is designed with a RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) knowledge base and system prompt architecture. Examples in both code, text, and images are provided in the LLM responses.

</details>


### [87] [A Graph Neural Network Model for Real-Time Gesture Recognition Based on sEMG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07850)

Abstract: For seemless control of advanced hand prostheses and augmented reality, accurate and immediate hand gestures recognition is essential. Surface electromyography (sEMG) signals obtained from the forearm are commonly employed for this purpose. In this paper, we present a novel approach for sEMG representation that utilizes graph networks which contain information about muscle activation patterns in the forearm. Based on these graph networks, we have developed a machine learning algorithm capable of real-time hand gesture recognition using a graph neural network. The algorithm's performance was evaluated using sEMG signals acquired from myoband, which has 8 electrodes placed around the forearm, involving 8 healthy subjects. The proposed method demonstrated an average classification accuracy of 99\%, surpassing the performance of state-of-the-art techniques. The average time for both graph construction and prediction stood at 48ms utilizing a M1 pro CPU, rendering the approach well-suited for real-time applications.

</details>


### [88] [Agentic AI and Retrieval-Augmented Models in Straight-Through Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07858)
*Robert Richardson,Josh Meyers,Brian Hartman,David Sandberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨AI（特别是LLM、RAG、多智能体系统）在精算承保流程中的应用，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文主题是精算科学（actuarial science）中的承保流程自动化，涉及LLM、RAG、多智能体系统。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或边缘部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。任务为精算承保（underwriting）中的直通式决策（straight-through decision），场景为小型商业企业主保单（Business Owner Policies, BOPs）。传感器类型、目标类别均不适用。

Model architecture: 涉及三种架构：(i) 单LLM基线, (ii) 朴素RAG系统, (iii) 多智能体"Agentic RAG"流水线（结合定向检索、第三方数据检查、显式多步规则评估）。与遥感目标检测模型架构无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Experiments: 比较三种承保流水线，Agentic RAG在多步和缺失信息场景下表现最佳。与遥感目标检测实验无关。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于精算AI应用领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is beginning to reshape actuarial practice, particularly in domains that require reasoning over unstructured documents, heterogeneous data sources, and regulated decision workflows. Actuaries now face a design space that ranges from traditional rule-based automation to large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and multi-agent ``agentic'' systems that plan, retrieve, call tools, and reflect. This paper examines how these emerging architectures can support actuarial priorities such as transparency, auditability, and human-in-the-loop governance, with a focus on straight-through decision processes. To make these ideas concrete, we develop and analyze an agentic AI framework for straight-through underwriting of small commercial Business Owner Policies (BOPs). We construct a synthetic but realistic experimental environment and compare three underwriting pipelines: (i) a single-LLM baseline, (ii) a naive RAG system, and (iii) a multi-agent ``Agentic RAG'' pipeline that combines targeted retrieval, third-party data checks, and explicit multi-step rule evaluation. The agentic system performs best overall, with the largest gains in multi-step and missing-information scenarios, where structured retrieval and reflection help the model avoid unsupported straight-through decisions.

</details>


### [89] [Feedback Manipulation Regularization: Enabling Offline Agent Alignment for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07859)
*Benjamin Poole,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种反馈操纵正则化方法(FMR)，利用评估性反馈作为纠正信号来改进模仿学习策略的对齐性，在Safety Gymnasium环境中验证了最高98%的对齐错误率降低。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于强化学习中的对齐问题（alignment），涉及模仿学习和反馈信号，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：强化学习策略对齐（RL alignment）；应用场景：Safety Gymnasium安全环境；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出的是算法无关（algorithm-agnostic）的正则化方法FMR，可应用于多种模仿学习算法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何部署相关指标，如参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等。

Datasets and metrics: 实验环境：Safety Gymnasium（安全对齐评估测试平台）；评价指标：对齐错误率（misalignment reduction，最高98%）。

Experiments: 在Safety Gymnasium环境中，FMR方法在多种模仿学习算法上均表现出改进，在有限数据场景下仍保持鲁棒性，最高可减少98%的对齐错误。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅在Safety Gymnasium仿真环境中验证，未涉及真实物理系统或视觉感知任务；3. 未讨论计算效率或部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于强化学习对齐领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) research has increasingly shifted focus towards alignment, ensuring agents learn behaviors adhering to human values. While human demonstrations and feedback have proven crucial for alignment, existing approaches predominantly combine these signals using multi-stage pipelines designed for the contextual bandit framing of language generation. Yet little work explores how these complementary inputs can serve as a richer, interconnected signal for single-stage offline training in fully sequential decision-making environments. We propose Feedback Manipulation Regularization (FMR), an algorithm-agnostic method that harnesses evaluative feedback as a corrective signal to improve the alignment of imitation learning policies. We adapt Safety Gymnasium environments to be a principled testbed for alignment evaluation, demonstrating improved aptitude and up to a 98\% reduction in misalignment across a range of imitation learning algorithms. FMR remains robust in limited data regimes, even when learning from scarce aligned and uninformative noisy demonstrations.

</details>


### [90] [Nigeria Machinery: A Low-Resource Industrial Dataset with a Domain-Grounded Reasoning Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07883)
*Gospel Bassey,Vincent Fakiyesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个尼日利亚工业机械使用与故障数据集（89条记录），并提出了基于链式推理（CoT）的数值推理示例构建方法，用于提升语言模型在工业数值任务上的领域接地性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或部署技术。论文聚焦于非洲工业机械数据集的构建和语言模型的数值推理能力提升，属于自然语言处理（NLP）和工业数据分析领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：工业机械数据集的构建与链式推理（CoT）示例生成；应用场景：尼日利亚制造业和油气行业的数值分析；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：工业机械（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，提及使用语言模型（language models）构建数据集和生成推理示例。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Nigeria Machinery Usage and Failures Dataset（89条机器级记录，28个指标，覆盖2006-2025年）；评价指标：领域接地性（domain-grounded prompts比例从1/78提升至94/94）、检索答案匹配率（84/84）。

Experiments: 主要结果：通过提出的CoT推理示例构建方法，领域接地性提示比例从1/78提升至94/94，检索答案全部匹配源值（84/84）。

Limitations: 数据集规模极小（89条记录，17个指标仅有一个观测值），作者明确说明这是参考和种子数据集而非大规模训练集；大多数推理行为是检索而非多步计算。

Ideas for my research: 与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接可借鉴点。如果强行关联，论文中提到的"领域接地性"（domain grounding）概念在遥感领域也有一定意义——即确保模型训练数据与真实遥感场景分布一致，避免数据偏差。但整体而言，该论文的方法、任务和场景与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化差距过大。

Abstract: There is relatively little, public, and model-ready data on industrial machinery for African economies. This makes it hard to do quantitative analysis or to train language models on numeric tasks grounded in that setting. We release two things to help with part of this problem. The first is the Nigeria Machinery Usage and Failures Dataset: 89 machine-level records across 28 indicators, covering Nigeria's manufacturing and oil and gas sectors from 2006 to 2025. Every record names a public source and is decoded by a codebook. The second is a method for building chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning examples from these sparse numeric values. The result is 94 prompt, completion, and reasoning-trace rows. In every row, the prompt names the real indicator, subsector, year, and source of the record it comes from. The data adaptation work was carried out by Adaption Labs. Along the way we describe a problem that is common when language models are used to build datasets. The prompts can match the real numbers while saying nothing about the real domain. We show that fixing this raises the share of domain-grounded prompts from 1 out of 78 in an earlier release to 94 out of 94, and that every retrieval answer now matches its source value (84 out of 84). We release the data, the reasoning layer, and a per-row provenance file under CC-BY-4.0. We are clear about the limits. With 89 records and 17 indicators that have only one observation, this is a reference and seed dataset, not a large training set. Most reasoning rows are retrieval rather than multi-step computation.

</details>


### [91] [Persona Cartography: Charting Language Model Personality Traits in Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07916)
*Luke Baines,Anton Gonzalvez Hawthorne,Mariia Koroliuk,Irakli Shalibashvili,Clément Dumas,Konstantinos Voudouris,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用OCEAN人格框架（大五人格）来描述和控制大语言模型的行为模式（personas），通过低秩适配器（LoRA）放大或抑制特定人格特质，并验证了这些特质轴对安全相关行为的影响。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化，而是聚焦于大语言模型的人格建模与控制。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM人格建模与行为控制；应用场景：大语言模型安全与行为对齐；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 使用低秩适配器（LoRA）对LLM进行微调以控制人格特质；涉及6个模型（4B-32B参数规模，来自3个模型家族）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。LoRA本身是一种参数高效微调方法，但本文并未从模型压缩或加速角度讨论轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。模型规模为4B-32B参数，远超出星上部署的可行范围。

Datasets and metrics: 使用LLM-judge（经人工验证面板校准）、特质特异性多选题基准、标准能力评估作为评价指标；数据集摘要未具体说明。

Experiments: 在6个模型（4B-32B，3个模型家族）上实验，发现适配器对目标特质的移动大致单调、可加性组合、在中等尺度下保持能力基准性能；神经质和宜人性轴分别影响挫败感和谄媚行为。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何相关技术借鉴。论文本身未讨论模型压缩、推理加速、边缘部署等话题。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。本文属于LLM安全与对齐方向，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit recurring behavioural patterns -- personas -- that shape generalisation and safety, but we lack reliable tools for decomposing, measuring, and controlling them. Our central insight is to treat personas as positions in a space of behavioural traits, using the OCEAN framework to describe model personas in terms of Openness, Conscientiousness, Extraversion, Agreeableness, and Neuroticism. We train low-rank adapters to amplify or suppress individual traits, and evaluate their effects using an LLM-judge calibrated against a human-validated panel, trait-specific multiple-choice benchmarks, and standard capability evaluations. Across six models from three families (4B-32B), we find that each adapter moves its target trait largely monotonically with scale, combines approximately additively with other adapters to construct mixed personas, and preserves performance on capability benchmarks at moderate scales. We further show that the induced trait axes affect safety-relevant behaviour in downstream evaluations: for example, moving along neuroticism and agreeableness axes affects frustration and sycophancy respectively. We also introduce an unsupervised psychometric pipeline that recovers four interpretable behavioural factors (tone, initiative, didacticism, epistemic caution) from model rollouts. Persona control can then be considered in terms of learning, scaling, and composing traits in weight space, providing a bridge between personality measurement, model editing, and safety.

</details>


### [92] [Evaluating the Effect of Frame Rate in Sequence-Based Classification of Autism-Related Self-Stimulatory Hand Idiosyncrasies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07957)
*Raunak Mondal,Peter Washington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于视频的自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)相关自我刺激行为的自动检测，使用LSTM/GRU/I3D等时序模型在SSBD数据集上进行行为分类。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视频行为分类（自我刺激行为检测）；场景：临床自闭症筛查；传感器：普通视频摄像头；目标类别：自我刺激行为（非遥感目标）。摘要未说明是否涉及遥感、卫星、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、航空图像等。

Model architecture: LSTM、GRU、I3D（用于迁移学习）；特征提取基于pose-derived features。摘要未说明轻量网络设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、边缘部署或星上部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Self-Stimulatory Behavior Diagnosis (SSBD) dataset；评价指标：accuracy（准确率）、mean loss；实验设置：不同帧采样间隔（1,5,15,30,45,90帧）、数据增强消融实验、个性化机器学习（per-subject temporal split）。

Experiments: LSTM和GRU在15帧采样间隔下分别达到97.5%和98.75%的峰值准确率，超过此前CNN基线（62-76%）；数据增强消融实验中，水平翻转最高独立准确率48.78%，上采样缺失导致最大性能下降；个性化模型平均损失1.84，标准差0.79。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关：1）非遥感场景；2）非目标检测任务；3）无轻量化方法；4）无部署相关指标；5）数据集SSBD为临床行为视频，非遥感图像/卫星数据。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的是其对时序采样策略（不同帧间隔）和视频数据增强的系统性消融实验设计思路，但与本课题（遥感在轨目标检测轻量化）方向差异过大。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) affects over 75 million individuals worldwide, yet scalable computational methods for remote behavioral screening remain limited. This study addresses two complementary challenges in automated detection of autism-related self-stimulatory behaviors from video: (1) identifying the optimal sequence-based neural network architecture and temporal sampling rate, and (2) characterizing data augmentation strategies for training on small behavioral datasets. For the first objective, long short-term memory (LSTM) and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models were trained on pose-derived features from the Self-Stimulatory Behavior Diagnosis (SSBD) dataset at frame sampling intervals of 1, 5, 15, 30, 45, and 90 frames. Both architectures exceeded prior convolutional neural network (CNN) baselines (62-76% accuracy), with peak accuracies of 97.5% (LSTM) and 98.75% (GRU) at a sampling interval of every 15 frames. For the second objective, ten data augmentation strategies were applied to an I3D transfer learning pipeline, with an ablation study quantifying the marginal contribution of each technique. Horizontal flip achieved the highest standalone accuracy (48.78%), while exclusion of upsampling from the augmentation pipeline produced the largest performance degradation, indicating its necessity for complex behavioral video augmentation. A personalized machine learning approach, in which per-subject models were trained and tested on temporally split segments of each video, produced consistent predictions (mean loss 1.84, SD 0.79). These results provide practitioners with concrete guidance on architecture selection, sampling rate, and augmentation strategy for video-based behavioral classification in data-scarce clinical domains.

</details>


### [93] [Agentic Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07984)
*Seokhoon Jeong,Mijung Kim,Taehwan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentNAS，利用LLM生成种子架构并分解为"槽位架构"，自动定义搜索空间供传统NAS探索，在17个多模态任务上取得SOTA。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关系。论文聚焦于通用NAS方法改进（LLM+NAS协作），任务涵盖分类、回归、分割、多标签标注，不涉及遥感场景或目标检测任务，也未讨论模型轻量化或部署问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对LLM辅助NAS的通用方法论感兴趣，可作为旁路参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 通用机器学习任务（分类、密集回归、分割、多标签标注），模态多样（NAS-Bench-360和Unseen NAS上的17个任务），不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测场景。

Model architecture: AgentNAS三阶段流水线：LLM生成种子架构 → 分解为槽位架构（含命名可互换模块槽） → 传统NAS在自动定义的搜索空间中搜索。核心是LLM与NAS的分工协作机制。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: NAS-Bench-360和Unseen NAS上的17个任务，涵盖分类、回归、分割、多标签标注。评价指标为各任务的标准指标（具体名称摘要未列出）。

Experiments: 在17个任务中11个取得SOTA，超越包括任务特定专家设计在内的基线方法。消融实验表明LLM种子和NAS搜索互补：LLM种子已超越多数任务基线，NAS通过槽间组合搜索带来额外增益。该模式在三种不同能力的LLM上均成立。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论计算开销（LLM调用成本、NAS搜索成本）；3. 未涉及边缘/星上部署场景；4. 搜索空间虽自动定义但仍受限于槽位分解方式。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1) LLM辅助设计轻量检测模型架构的思路（用LLM生成种子架构，再在轻量化约束下搜索）；2) "槽位架构"思想可用于将遥感目标检测模型分解为特征提取、特征融合、检测头等可替换模块，分别进行轻量化搜索；3) 如果未来将搜索空间约束为低FLOPs/低参数量，该方法可能适用于星上模型自动设计。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) methods have grown increasingly efficient, yet they remain bounded by manually engineered search spaces that require substantial domain expertise and must be rebuilt for every new task. Large language models (LLMs) can generate architectures in an open-ended space, but how to optimally divide the labor between LLM-driven design and NAS-driven search remains unexplored. We propose a mechanism that bridges these two paradigms: an LLM produces a high-quality seed architecture, then decomposes it into a "slotted architecture", a scaffold with named, interchangeable module slots that automatically defines a bounded, task-specific search space for conventional NAS to explore, without manual engineering. We instantiate this mechanism in AgentNAS, a modular three-phase pipeline in which each component's contribution can be measured independently. On 17 tasks spanning classification, dense regression, segmentation, and multi-label tagging across diverse modalities (NAS-Bench-360 and Unseen NAS), AgentNAS establishes a new state of the art on 11 tasks, outperforming published baselines including task-specific expert designs. Ablation studies show that the two search mechanisms are broadly complementary: the LLM-generated seed already surpasses published baselines on the majority of tasks, and NAS delivers additional gains in most cases through combinatorial recombination across slots, a mode of search that independent LLM samples cannot replicate. These patterns hold across three LLMs of different capability levels, confirming that the division of labor is robust. Our code is available at https://github.com/alroimfebruary/AgentNAS.

</details>


### [94] [Concretized Proposition Prompting Resolves Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08018)
*Changhun Lee,Minguk Jeon,Jongkyung Shin,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Concretized Proposition Prompting (CPP)框架，通过显式具体化与问题相关的命题，解决大语言模型中组合性与知识性之间的权衡问题（Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy），提升推理性能。
